# Set up symlinks to directories whose contents got moved to /usr
for moved in bin lib lib64 sbin; do \
	test -d /build/glibc-build/testroot.pristine/usr/$moved || \
	  mkdir -p /build/glibc-build/testroot.pristine/usr/$moved ;\
	test -e /build/glibc-build/testroot.pristine/$moved || \
	  ln -s usr/$moved /build/glibc-build/testroot.pristine/$moved ;\
done
